all_indices = sorted(set(emb_map) | set(mkpp_map) | set(con_map) | set(sc_map))\\n\\n    merged = []\\n    for idx in all_indices:\\n        record = {\\n            \\\"index\\\": idx,\\n            \\\"benchmark\\\": benchmark,\\n            \\\"s1_embedding\\\": emb_map.get(idx, {}).get(\\\"s1_embedding\\\"),\\n            \\\"s2_minkpp\\\": mkpp_map.get(idx, {}).get(\\\"s2_minkpp\\\"),\\n ci is not None:\\n                s = f\\\"{v:.3f} [{ci[0]:.3f},{ci[1]:.3f}]\\\"\\n            elif v is not None:\\n                s = f\\\"{v:.3f}\\\"\\n            else:\\n                s = \\\"N/A\\\"\\n            parts.append(f\\\"{s:>{col_w}}\\\")\\n        print(\\\" \\\".join(parts))\\n\\n    print(\\\"=\\\" * len(header))\\n\\n    # Signal degradation summary\\n    for sig_key in SIGNAL_KEYS:\\n        if sig_key not in agg_auc or len(regime_names) < 2:\\n            continue\\n        first_rname = regime_names[0]\\n        last_rname = regime_names[-1]\\n        v_first = agg_auc[sig_key].get(first_rname)\\n        v_last = agg_auc[sig_key].get(last_rname)\\n        if v_first is not None and v_last is not None:\\n            delta = v_last - v_first\\n            label = sig_labels.get(sig_key, sig_key)\\n            print(f\\\"  {label}: {v_first:.3f} -> {v_last:.3f} (delta={delta:+.3f})\\\")\\n\\n    print()\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}}}

\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nSignal 1: Embedding-based near-duplicate retrieval.\\\\n\\\\nEncodes benchmark items and SFT training data with sentence-transformers,\\\\nbuilds a FAISS index, and computes max cosine similarity between each\\\\nbenchmark item and the top-K nearest training segments.\\\\n\\\\nInput:  Benchmark dataset, SFT training data (JSONL from exp-001)\\\\nOutput: JSON with per-item max cosine similarity scores\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport logging\\\\nimport os\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\n\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\\\n\\\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\\\n\\\\nHF_HOME = os.environ.get(\\\\\\\"HF_HOME\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\\\\\\")\\\\nos.environ[\\\\\\\"HF_HOME\\\\\\\"] = HF_HOME\\\\n\\\\nDEFAULT_ENCODER = \\\\\\\"sentence-transformers/all-mpnet-base-v2\\\\\\\"\\\\nDEFAULT_TOP_K = 10\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_sft_texts(data_path: str) -> list[str]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Extract text content from SFT JSONL training data.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    texts = []\\\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\\\n        for line in f:\\\\n            row = json.loads(line)\\\\n            messages = row.get(\\\\\\\"messages\\\\\\\", [])\\\\n            if isinstance(messages, str):\\\\n                messages = json.loads(messages)\\\\n            parts = []\\\\n            for msg in messages:\\\\n                if msg.get(\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\") in (\\\\\\\"user\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"assistant\\\\\\\"):\\\\n                    parts.append(msg[\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\"])\\\\n            if parts:\\\\n                texts.append(\\\\\\\" \\\\\\\".join(parts))\\\\n    return texts\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_benchmark_texts(benchmark: str, cache_dir: str) -> list[dict]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Load benchmark items as text strings with item metadata.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\\\n\\\\n    items = []\\\\n    if benchmark == \\\\\\\"mmlu\\\\\\\":\\\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\\\\\"cais/mmlu\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"all\\\\\\\", split=\\\\\\\"test\\\\\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\\\n        for i, row in enumerate(ds):\\\\n            choices = \\\\\\\" \\\\\\\".join(f\\\\\\\"({chr(65+j)}) {c}\\\\\\\" for j, c in enumerate(row[\\\\\\\"choices\\\\\\\"]))\\\\n            text = f\\\\\\\"{row['question']} {choices}\\\\\\\"\\\\n            items.append({\\\\\\\"index\\\\\\\": i, \\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\": text, \\\\\\\"benchmark\\\\\\\": benchmark})\\\\n    elif benchmark == \\\\\\\"gsm8k\\\\\\\":\\\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\\\\\"openai/gsm8k\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"main\\\\\\\", split=\\\\\\\"test\\\\\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\\\n        for i, row in enumerate(ds):\\\\n            items.append({\\\\\\\"index\\\\\\\": i, \\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\": row[\\\\\\\"question\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"benchmark\\\\\\\": benchmark})\\\\n    elif benchmark == \\\\\\\"arc_challenge\\\\\\\":\\\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\\\\\"allenai/ai2_arc\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"ARC-Challenge\\\\\\\", split=\\\\\\\"test\\\\\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\\\n        for i, row in enumerate(ds):\\\\n            choices = \\\\\\\" \\\\\\\".join(f\\\\\\\"({l}) {t}\\\\\\\" for l, t in zip(row[\\\\\\\"choices\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"label\\\\\\\"], row[\\\\\\\"choices\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\"]))\\\\n            text = f\\\\\\\"{row['question']} {choices}\\\\\\\"\\\\n            items.append({\\\\\\\"index\\\\\\\": i, \\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\": text, \\\\\\\"benchmark\\\\\\\": benchmark})\\\\n    elif benchmark == \\\\\\\"humaneval\\\\\\\":\\\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\\\\\"openai/openai_humaneval\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"openai_humaneval\\\\\\\", split=\\\\\\\"test\\\\\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\\\n        for i, row in enumerate(ds):\\\\n            items.append({\\\\\\\"index\\\\\\\": i, \\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\": row[\\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\"], \\\\\\\"benchmark\\\\\\\": benchmark})\\\\n    else:\\\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\\\\\"Unknown benchmark: {benchmark}\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return items\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef encode_texts(texts: list[str], encoder_name: str, cache_dir: str,\\\\n                 batch_size: int = 64, device: str = \\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\") -> np.ndarray:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Encode texts into normalized embeddings.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer\\\\n\\\\n    model = SentenceTransformer(encoder_name, cache_folder=cache_dir, device=device)\\\\n    embeddings = model.encode(\\\\n        texts, batch_size=batch_size, show_progress_bar=True,\\\\n        normalize_embeddings=True, convert_to_numpy=True,\\\\n    )\\\\n    return embeddings\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef build_faiss_index(embeddings: np.ndarray, use_gpu: bool = True):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Build a FAISS index for cosine similarity search (on normalized vectors, IP = cosine).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    import faiss\\\\n\\\\n    dim = embeddings.shape[1]\\\\n    index = faiss.IndexFlatIP(dim)\\\\n    if use_gpu and faiss.get_num_gpus() > 0:\\\\n        res = faiss.StandardGpuResources()\\\\n        index = faiss.index_cpu_to_gpu(res, 0, index)\\\\n    index.add(embeddings.astype(np.float32))\\\\n    return index\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_embedding_signal(\\\\n    benchmark: str,\\\\n    sft_data_path: str,\\\\n    cache_dir: str,\\\\n    encoder_name: str = DEFAULT_ENCODER,\\\\n    top_k: int = DEFAULT_TOP_K,\\\\n    batch_size: int = 64,\\\\n    device: str = \\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\",\\\\n) -> list[dict]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Compute per-item max cosine similarity with training data.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Loading SFT training data from %s\\\\\\\", sft_data_path)\\\\n    train_texts = load_sft_texts(sft_data_path)\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Training segments: %d\\\\\\\", len(train_texts))\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Loading benchmark items: %s\\\\\\\", benchmark)\\\\n    bench_items = load_benchmark_texts(benchmark, cache_dir)\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Benchmark items: %d\\\\\\\", len(bench_items))\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Encoding training data with %s\\\\\\\", encoder_name)\\\\n    train_embeddings = encode_texts(train_texts, encoder_name, cache_dir, batch_size, device)\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Encoding benchmark items\\\\\\\")\\\\n    bench_texts = [item[\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\"] for item in bench_items]\\\\n    bench_embeddings = encode_texts(bench_texts, encoder_name, cache_dir, batch_size, device)\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Building FAISS index (%d vectors, dim=%d)\\\\\\\", len(train_embeddings), train_embeddings.shape[1])\\\\n    try:\\\\n        index = build_faiss_index(train_embeddings, use_gpu=True)\\\\n    except Exception:\\\\n        logger.warning(\\\\\\\"FAISS GPU failed, falling back to CPU\\\\\\\")\\\\n        index = build_faiss_index(train_embeddings, use_gpu=False)\\\\n\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Searching top-%d nearest neighbors\\\\\\\", top_k)\\\\n    scores, indices = index.search(bench_embeddings.astype(np.float32), top_k)\\\\n\\\\n    results = []\\\\n    for i, item in enumerate(bench_items):\\\\n        max_sim = float(scores[i, 0])  # already sorted descending\\\\n        mean_sim = float(np.mean(scores[i]))\\\\n        results.append({\\\\n            \\\\\\\"index\\\\\\\": item[\\\\\\\"index\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            \\\\\\\"benchmark\\\\\\\": item[\\\\\\\"benchmark\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            \\\\\\\"s1_embedding\\\\\\\": max_sim,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"s1_embedding_mean_topk\\\\\\\": mean_sim,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"s1_top_k_sims\\\\\\\": [float(s) for s in scores[i]],\\\\n        })\\\\n\\\\n    return results\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef main():\\\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\\\\\"Signal 1: Embedding near-duplicate retrieval\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--benchmark\\\\\\\", type=str, required=True,\\\\n                        choices=[\\\\\\\"mmlu\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"gsm8k\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"arc_challenge\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"humaneval\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--sft-data-path\\\\\\\", type=str, required=True,\\\\n                        help=\\\\\\\"Path to SFT training JSONL (from exp-001 prepare_sft_data.py)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--output-dir\\\\\\\", type=str, required=True)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--encoder\\\\\\\", type=str, default=DEFAULT_ENCODER,\\\\n                        help=\\\\\\\"Sentence-transformers model name\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--top-k\\\\\\\", type=int, default=DEFAULT_TOP_K)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--batch-size\\\\\\\", type=int, default=64)\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--gpu\\\\\\\", type=str, default=None,\\\\n                        help=\\\\\\\"GPU device ID (e.g., '0')\\\\\\\")\\\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--cache-dir\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\\\\\\")\\\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\\\n\\\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\\\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    if args.gpu:\\\\n        os.environ[\\\\\\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\\\\\\"] = args.gpu\\\\n\\\\n    device = \\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\"\\\\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\\\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\\\n\\\\n    results = compute_embedding_signal(\\\\n        benchmark=args.benchmark,\\\\n        sft_data_path=args.sft_data_path,\\\\n        cache_dir=args.cache_dir,\\\\n        encoder_name=args.encoder,\\\\n        top_k=args.top_k,\\\\n        batch_size=args.batch_size,\\\\n        device=device,\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    out_file = output_dir / f\\\\\\\"signal_embedding_{args.benchmark}.json\\\\\\\"\\\\n    with open(out_file, \\\\\\\"w\\\\\\\") as f:\\\\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\\\\n    logger.info(\\\\\\\"Saved %d items to %s\\\\\\\", len(results), out_file)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nif __name__ == \\\\\\\"__main__\\\\\\\":\\\\n    main()\\\\n\\\", f\\\"Question: {row['question']}\\\\n{choices}\\\\nAnswer: {row['answerKey']}\\\"\\n    elif benchmark == \\\"humaneval\\\":\\n        return row[\\\"prompt\\\"] + (row.get(\\\"canonical_solution\\\", np.ndarray:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute per-token log probabilities for input text.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    inputs = tokenizer(text, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\", truncation=True, max_length=max_length)\\n    input_ids = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].to(model.device)\\n\\n    with torch.no_grad():\\n        outputs = model(input_ids)\\n    logits = outputs.logits\\n\\n    # Shift: predict token t from position t-1\\n    shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :]\\n    shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:]\\n\\n    log_probs = torch.log_softmax(shift_logits, dim=-1)\\n    token_log_probs = log_probs.gather(2, shift_labels.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)\\n\\n    return token_log_probs[0].cpu().float().numpy()\\n\\n\\ndef minkpp_score(target_logprobs: np.ndarray, ref_logprobs: np.ndarray,\\n                 k_percent: int = DEFAULT_K_PERCENT) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Min-K%++ score: z-normalize target log-probs against reference,\\n    then take the mean of the k% lowest values.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    n_tokens = min(len(target_logprobs), len(ref_logprobs))\\n    if n_tokens == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n\\n    target\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 736 字符] ...\\n\\nin a benchmark.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    # Load benchmark\\n    if benchmark == \\\"mmlu\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"cais/mmlu\\\", \\\"all\\\", split=\\\"test\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n    elif benchmark == \\\"gsm8k\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"openai/gsm8k\\\", \\\"main\\\", split=\\\"test\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n    elif benchmark == \\\"arc_challenge\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/ai2_arc\\\", \\\"ARC-Challenge\\\", split=\\\"test\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n    elif benchmark == \\\"humaneval\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"openai/openai_humaneval\\\", \\\"openai_humaneval\\\", split=\\\"test\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown benchmark: {benchmark}\\\")\\n\\n    # Load target model\\n    target_model, target_tok = load_model_and_tokenizer(target_model_path, cache_dir)\\n\\n    # Compute target log-probs for all items\\n    logger.info(\\\"Computing target model log-probs (%d items)\\\", len(ds))\\n    target_item_logprobs = []\\n    item_texts = []\\n    for row in tqdm(ds, desc=\\\"Target log-probs\\\"):\\n        text = format_benchmark_item(row, benchmark)\\n        item_texts.append(text)\\n        lp = compute_token_logprobs(target_model, target_tok, text, max_length)\\n        target_item_logprobs.append(lp)\\n\\n    # Free target model memory\\n    del target_model\\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\\n\\n    # Load reference model\\n    ref_model, ref_tok = load_model_and_tokenizer(ref_model_path, cache_dir)\\n\\n    # Compute reference log-probs\\n    logger.info(\\\"Computing reference model log-probs (%d items)\\\", len(ds))\\n    ref_item_logprobs = []\\n    for text in tqdm(item_texts, desc=\\\"Reference log-probs\\\"):\\n        lp = compute_token_logprobs(ref_model, ref_tok, text, max_length)\\n        ref_item_logprobs.append(lp)\\n\\n    del ref_model\\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\\n\\n    # Compute Min-K%++ scores\\n    results = []\\n    for i in range(len(ds)):\\n        score = minkpp_score(target_item_logprobs[i], ref_item_logprobs[i], k_percent)\\n        # Negate: lower Min-K%++ -> more likely member -> higher contamination suspicion\\n        results.append({\\n            \\\"index\\\": i,\\n            \\\"benchmark\\\": benchmark,\\n            \\\"s2_minkpp\\\": -score,\\n            \\\"s2_minkpp_raw\\\": score,\\n            \\\"n_tokens\\\": len(target_item_logprobs[i]),\\n        })\\n\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Signal 2: Min-K%++ membership inference\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--benchmark\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n                        choices=[\\\"mmlu\\\", \\\"gsm8k\\\", \\\"arc_challenge\\\", \\\"humaneval\\\"])\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model-path\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n (clean) model path, e.g. 0%% dosage checkpoint\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output-dir\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--k-percent\\\", type=int, default=DEFAULT_K_PERCENT,\\n                        help=\\\"K%% for Min-K%% selection (default: 20)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--max-length\\\", type=int, default=2048)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--cache-dir\\\", type=str, default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\")\\n\\n    if args.gpu:\\n        os.environ[\\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\\"] = args.gpu\\n\\n    output_dir )\\n\\n    out_file = output_dir / f\\\"signal_minkpp_{args.benchmark}.json\\\"\\n    with open(out_file, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Saved %d Per-item accuracy is averaged over seeds\\n    (3 seeds -> possible values: 0, 0.33, 0.67, 1.0).\\n    Gap = mean(original) - mean(paraphrased).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(original_eval_paths) != len(paraphrase_eval_paths):\\n        raise ValueError(\\n            f\\\"Mismatched seed counts: {len(original_eval_paths)} original vs \\\"\\n            f\\\"{len(paraphrase_eval_paths)} paraphrased\\\"\\n        )\\n\\n    # Load correctness vectors for all seeds\\n    orig_seeds = []\\n    for path in original_eval_paths:\\n        orig_seeds.append(load_per_item_correctness(path, benchmark))\\n\\n    para_seeds = []\\n    for path in paraphrase_eval_paths:\\n        para_seeds.append(load_per_item_correctness(path, benchmark))\\n\\n    n_items = len(orig_seeds[0])\\n    for i, vec in enumerate(orig_seeds + para_seeds):\\n        if len(vec) != n_items:\\n            raise ValueError(f\\\"Item count mismatch: expected {n_items}, got {len(vec)}\\\")\\n\\n    orig_matrix = np.array(orig_seeds, dtype=float)  # (n_seeds, n_items)\\n    para_matrix = np.array(para_seeds, dtype=float)\\n\\n    orig_mean = orig_matrix.mean(axis=0)  # (n_items,)\\n    para_mean = para_matrix.mean(axis=0)\\n    gap = orig_mean - para_mean\\n\\n    results = []\\n    for i in range(n_items):\\n        results.append({\\n            \\\"index\\\": i,\\n            \\\"benchmark\\\": benchmark,\\n            \\\"s3_constat\\\": float(gap[i]),\\n            \\\"s3_orig_accuracy\\\": float(orig_mean[i]),\\n            \\\"s3_para_accuracy\\\": float(para_mean[i]),\\n        })\\n\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Signal 3: ConStat performance gap\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--benchmark\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n                        choices=[\\\"mmlu\\\", \\\"gsm8k\\\", \\\"arc_challenge\\\", \\\"humaneval\\\"])\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--original-eval-paths\\\", type=str, nargs=\\\"+\\\", required=True,\\n                        help=\\\"Paths to eval_results.json for original items (one per seed)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--paraphrase-eval-paths\\\", type=str, nargs=\\\"+\\\", required=True,\\n                        help=\\\"Paths to eval_results.json for paraphrased items (one per seed)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output-dir\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\")\\n\\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    results = compute_constat_signal(\\n        benchmark=args.benchmark,\\n        original_eval_paths=args.original_eval_paths,\\n        paraphrase_eval_paths=args.paraphrase_eval_paths,\\n    )\\n\\n    out_file = output_dir / f\\\"signal_constat_{args.benchmark}.json\\\"\\n    with open(out_file, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Saved %d items to %s\\\", len(results), out_file)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.4}}"}}}

else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown benchmark: {benchmark}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef compute_selfcritique_signal(\\n    benchmark: str,\\n    model_path: str,\\n    cache_dir: str,\\n    max_answer_tokens: int = 64,\\n) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute self-critique entropy for all items in a benchmark.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    if benchmark == \\\"mmlu\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"cais/mmlu\\\", \\\"all\\\", split=\\\"test\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n    elif benchmark == \\\"gsm8k\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"openai/gsm8k\\\", \\\"main\\\", split=\\\"test\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n    elif benchmark == \\\"arc_challenge\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/ai2_arc\\\", \\\"ARC-Challenge\\\", split=\\\"test\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n    elif benchmark == \\\"humaneval\\\":\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"openai/openai_humaneval\\\", \\\"openai_humaneval\\\", split=\\\"test\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown benchmark: {benchmark}\\\")\\n\\n    model, tokenizer = load_model_and_tokenizer(model_path, cache_dir)\\n\\n    tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n    tokenizer.padding_side = 'left'\\n\\n    yes_ids, no_ids = _get_yes_no_token_ids(tokenizer)\\n\\n    items = list(ds)\\n    results = []\\n    for batch_start in tqdm(range(0, len(items), BATCH_SIZE), desc=f\\\"Self-critique {benchmark}\\\"):\\n        batch_items = items[batch_start:batch_start + BATCH_SIZE]\\n\\n        q_prompts = [format_question_prompt(row, benchmark) for row in batch_items]\\n        answers = batch_generate_answers(model, tokenizer, q_prompts, max_answer_tokens)\\n\\n        critique_prompts = \\\"s4_yes_prob\\\": ent_data[\\\"yes_prob\\\"],\\n                \\\"s4_no_prob\\\": ent_data[\\\"no_prob\\\"],\\n            })\\n\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = choices=[\\\"mmlu\\\", \\\"gsm8k\\\", \\\"arc_challenge\\\", \\\"humaneval\\\"])\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model-path\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n                        help=\\\"Target model path\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output-dir\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--max-answer-tokens\\\", type=int, default=64)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--cache-dir\\\", type=str, default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\\")\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n
